# Skills · S · part 47

882 entries, sdd-cascade to sdd-parallel. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **sdd-cascade**: Carry a large refactoring, or a new system whose algorithm must be invented, through a short chain of standalone skills — survey the code with the project's tools, optionally source the rules, agree the algorithm, agree the structure, translate it into code in a fresh agent, review by running the project's checks. Three gates are the owner's word; decisions live in one register. Use when the lifecycle marks a task :path :cascade, when the user invokes sdd-cascade or /sdd-cascade, or when a cascaded task must be resumed. ([zakyrion/sdd-flow](https://github.com/zakyrion/sdd-flow/tree/HEAD/templates/skills/sdd-cascade) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **sdd-catchup**: Produce a colleague-style catch-up briefing on the SDD project. Threads recent activity, active work, and warm gaps into 2–4 story-arc clusters with action-tight numbered items. Use as the check-in mode when starting a session or whenever a fresh briefing is wanted. ([esdete2/tailwind-rainbow](https://github.com/esdete2/tailwind-rainbow/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/sdd-catchup) · ★55)

- **sdd-close**: This skill should be used when the user asks to "close out REQ-…", "close out PR N", "write the PR body", "mark the PR ready", "the PR is merged, clean up", or "remove the merged branch and its worktree".
